K\supseteq J\end{subarray}}(-1)^{|K|-|J|}\bigr){\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=\\
=\bigotimes_{a\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}.

(15)

Note that

{\mathscr{R}}(A,I)\supseteq{\mathscr{R}}^{\prime}(A,I),\qquad{\mathscr{P}}(I)^{A}\supseteq{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)^{A},

and

{\mathscr{R}}(A,I)\setminus{\mathscr{R}}^{\prime}(A,I)={\mathscr{P}}(I)^{A}\setminus{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)^{A}

as subsets of
{\mathscr{P}}(I)^{A}
.
Thus

\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{R}}^{\prime}(A,I)}T(k)=\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{R}}(A,I)}T(k)-\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)^{A}}T(k)+\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)^{A}}T(k)=

(by
Lemma 15.1
and
equalities
(
14
)
and
(
15
))

=\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-|J|}\bigotimes_{a\in A}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-\bigotimes_{a\in A}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}I{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}+\bigotimes_{a\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=\\
=-\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}\bigotimes_{a\in A}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}+\bigotimes_{a\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}J{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}},

as required.
∎

§ 16. Chained monoids

Let
P
be a monoid.
Then
\langle P\rangle
is its monoid ring.
We call the monoid
P

chained
if

\langle P\rangle
is equipped with a chain of left ideals
\langle P\rangle^{[s]}
,

\langle P\rangle=\langle P\rangle^{[0]}\supseteq\langle P\rangle^{[1]}\supseteq\dotso.

Given a finite set
I
,
we
consider
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
as a monoid with respect to intersection
and
chain it by letting
\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[s]}
be
the subgroup generated by elements

\omega_{J}=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}K{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}},

where
J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
|J|\geqslant s
.

§ 17. The filtration
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

Let
P
be a chained monoid.
Let

\textstyle\sib T

and

\textstyle\sib Z

be pointed simplicial sets.
Let
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}
denote the set of based morphisms
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
.
Let
P
act on

\textstyle\sib Z

(on the left;
preserving the basepoint).
For an element
p\in P
,
let

p_{({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}

be its action.
(We will use this notation for all actions.)
The set
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}
carries the induced action of
P
.
Thus
the abelian group
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle
becomes a
(left)
module over
\langle P\rangle
.
We define a filtration

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle=\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[0]}\supseteq\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[1]}\supseteq\dotso.

Let

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}
,
j\in(n)
,
be pointed simplicial sets
and

{\sib f}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}

be a based morphism.
We have
the
{\mathbb{Z}}
-multilinear operation

\mathop{\sbighash}_{j\in(n)}:\prod_{j\in(n)}\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle\to\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle,\qquad\mathop{\sbighash}_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\sib v}_{j}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.86108pt\vskip 0.99025pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.99025pt\hrule height=0.86108pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.6458pt\vskip 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 1.07639pt\hrule height=0.6458pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.55974pt\vskip 0.90419pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.90419pt\hrule height=0.55974pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.38748pt\vskip 0.6458pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.6458pt\hrule height=0.38748pt}}}}_{j\in(n)}{\sib v}_{j}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}},

and
the homomorphism

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib f}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle\to\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle.

Take ensembles
v_{j}\in\langle P\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle
,
j\in(n)
,
and
consider the ensemble
v\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle
,

v=\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib f}\rangle\bigl(\mathop{\sbighash}_{j\in(n)}v_{j}\bigr).

(16)

We call
v
a
block
of
rank

s_{1}+\dotso+s_{n}
.
We let
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}\subseteq\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle
be the subgroup
generated by all blocks of rank at least
s
.
One easily sees that
it is a submodule.

Let

\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}
be a pointed simplicial set
and

{\sib k}:\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}
be a based simplicial morphism.
Then
the homomorphism

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib k}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle

takes
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}
to
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}
.
∎

Let

\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
be a pointed simplicial set
with an action of
P

and

{\sib h}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\to\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
be a
P
-equivariant based simplicial morphism.
Then
the homomorphism

\langle{\sib h}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle{\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle

takes
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}
to
\langle{\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}
.
∎

The cone
{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
carries the induced action of
P
.
We have the function

{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}},\qquad{\sib v}\mapsto{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib v}.

The homomorphism

\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle

takes
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}
to
\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}
.

It suffices to show that

\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle
sends any block to a block of the same rank.
Consider
the block (
16
).
Since

v_{j}\in\langle P\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle

and
the functions

{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\to({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}

preserve the action of
P
,
we have

\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(v_{j})\in\langle P\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle.

Let

{\sibr{in}}_{k}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{k}\to\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}

be the canonical insertions.
We have the commutative diagram

\textstyle{\bigvee\limits_{j\in(n)}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\sib e}:=\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.60275pt\vskip 0.69318pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.69318pt\hrule height=0.60275pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.45206pt\vskip 0.75346pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.75346pt\hrule height=0.45206pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.39182pt\vskip 0.63293pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.63293pt\hrule height=0.39182pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.27122pt\vskip 0.45206pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.45206pt\hrule height=0.27122pt}}}}\limits_{j\in(n)}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}\,{\sibr{in}}_{j}}
\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\sib g}}
\scriptstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib f}}
\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}(\bigvee\limits_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}),}

where

{\sib e}
is an isomorphism
(since
{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}
preserves wedges)
and

{\sib g}
is the unique lift of
{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib f}
.
For arbitrary based morphisms
{\sib v}_{j}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
,
we have the commutative diagram with sendings

\textstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\sib g}}
\textstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}}
\textstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}(\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j})},\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib f}}}
\scriptstyle{({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\sib e}}

\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.60275pt\vskip 0.69318pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.69318pt\hrule height=0.60275pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.45206pt\vskip 0.75346pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.75346pt\hrule height=0.45206pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.39182pt\vskip 0.63293pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.63293pt\hrule height=0.39182pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.27122pt\vskip 0.45206pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.45206pt\hrule height=0.27122pt}}}}\limits_{j\in(n)}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib v}_{j}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib f}(\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.60275pt\vskip 0.69318pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.69318pt\hrule height=0.60275pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.45206pt\vskip 0.75346pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.75346pt\hrule height=0.45206pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.39182pt\vskip 0.63293pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.63293pt\hrule height=0.39182pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.27122pt\vskip 0.45206pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.45206pt\hrule height=0.27122pt}}}}\limits_{j\in(n)}{\sib v}_{j}))}}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}(\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.60275pt\vskip 0.69318pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.69318pt\hrule height=0.60275pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.45206pt\vskip 0.75346pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.75346pt\hrule height=0.45206pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.39182pt\vskip 0.63293pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.63293pt\hrule height=0.39182pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.27122pt\vskip 0.45206pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.45206pt\hrule height=0.27122pt}}}}\limits_{j\in(n)}{\sib v}_{j}).}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

Thus
we have
the commutative diagram

\textstyle{\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\sib g}\rangle}
\textstyle{\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle}
\textstyle{\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}(\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j})}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib f}}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{(\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\sib e}\rangle}

and the sendings

\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(v_{j})}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib f}\rangle(\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}v_{j}))}}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}v_{j})}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

for our
(and
arbitrary)
ensembles
v_{j}
.
We get

\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(v)=\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle\bigl(\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\sib f}\rangle\bigl(\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}v_{j}\bigr)\bigr)=\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}})^{\sib g}\rangle\bigl(\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{j}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\rangle(v_{j})\bigr),

as promised.
∎

Let

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{i}
,

i\in(m)
,
be pointed simplicial sets
and

be ensembles.

\mathop{\sbighash}_{i\in(m)}v_{i}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\bigvee_{i\in(m)}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{i}}\rangle^{[s_{1}+\dotso+s_{m}]}.\displaymath@qed

Fissile and almost fissile ensembles.

Let
E
be a nonempy finite set.
An ensemble
q\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle
is called

fissile
if,
for any layout
A\in{\mathscr{A}}(E)
,

q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}

in
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}\rangle

(cf. §§ 2, 10).
It is called
r
-almost fissile
if,
for any layout
A\in{\mathscr{A}}(E)
,

\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}-q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}\rangle^{[r+1]}

(cf. § 13).

§ 18. The wedge
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)

Fix a finite set
I
.
Consider the pointed simplicial set

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)=\bigvee_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J).

Let

{\sibr{in}}_{J}:{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)

be the canonical insertions.

lead
vertex

is isolated.

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)
has the pointed simplicial subsets

and

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)=\bigvee_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(L)}{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J),\qquad L\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I).

For
J,K\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
J\supseteq K
,
let

{\svb t}^{J}_{K}:{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)\to{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus K)

be the morphism induced by the inclusion

.

Let the monoid
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
act on
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)
by the rule

\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\svb t}^{J}_{K\cap J}}
\scriptstyle{{\sibr{in}}_{J}}
\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus(K\cap J))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\sibr{in}}_{K\cap J}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{K_{({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I))}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I),}

K\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
The simplicial subsets

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)

and

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)

are
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-invariant.

For

and

,
we define a retraction
{\svb s}^{L}_{i}
by the commutative diagram

\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}\,{\sibr{in}}^{L}_{J}}
\scriptstyle{{\svb s}_{i}}
\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\svb s}^{L}_{i}}
\textstyle{{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\sibr{in}}^{L}_{J}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I),}

where

{\sibr{in}}^{L}_{J}
are the canonical insertions
and

are the canonical contractions (see § 11).
We call
{\svb s}^{L}_{i}
the canonical contraction,
too.
It follows from Lemma 11.1 that

is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant.

Given
a pointed simplicial set

\textstyle\sib T

,
introduce the
filling
function

\chi^{L}_{i}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}},\qquad{\sib v}\mapsto({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\xrightarrow{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\sib v}}{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)\xrightarrow{{\svb s}^{L}_{i}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)).

Since
{\svb s}^{L}_{i}
is a retraction,

\chi^{L}_{i}({\sib v})|_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}={\sib v}.

(17)

§ 19. The module
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle

Fix a finite set
I
.
We consider the
\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle
-modules
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle

for a number of pointed simplicial sets

\textstyle\sib T

.
For a
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-invariant pointed simplicial subset

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)
,
the subgroup
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\subseteq\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle
is a

\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle
-submodule.
If
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}
for two such subsets,
then

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}\subseteq\langle\widetilde{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

by Lemma 17.2.

For

L\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
,

i\in I\setminus L
,
and
a pointed simplicial set

\textstyle\sib T

,
the filling homomorphism

\langle\chi^{L}_{i}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle

takes

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}
.

By construction of
\chi^{L}_{i}
,
we have the decomposition

\langle\chi^{L}_{i}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)}\rangle}\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I))^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\langle({\svb s}^{L}_{i})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle}\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle.

By Lemma 17.3,

\langle{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)}\rangle
takes

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I))^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}
.
Since
{\svb s}^{L}_{i}
is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant,

\langle({\svb s}^{L}_{i})^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle
takes
the
\langle({\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I))^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{L}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\sibr c}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}}\rangle^{[s]}

by Lemma 17.2.
∎

Fix a nonempty finite set
E
.
For

F\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(E)

and

J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
introduce the based morphism

{\svb j}^{F}_{J}:(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}\to{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)

that takes
\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F
to the vertex

({\sibr{in}}_{I})_{[0]}(1_{{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)})
.

For

F\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(E)

and

J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,

\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{K}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

Since

{\svb j}^{F}_{K}=K_{({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})}({\svb j}^{F}_{J}),

the ensemble in question
equals
\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}

and
thus
belongs to
\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[|J|]}\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle
,
which is contained in
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle^{[|J|]}

by the definition of the latter.
∎

There exist fissile ensembles

p_{J}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle,\qquad J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I),

satisfying the following conditions
for each
J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
:

(1)

one has

p_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}

in
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}\rangle
;

(2)

one has

\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}p_{K}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

We will construct ensembles

p^{F}_{J}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle,\qquad(F,J)\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(I)\times{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I),

satisfying the following conditions

(0^{F}_{J})
,

(1^{F}_{J})
,
and

(2^{F}_{J})

for each pair
(F,J)\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(I)\times{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
:

(0^{F}_{J})

one has

p^{F}_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{J}

in
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}\rangle

for all
B\in{\mathscr{A}}(F)
;

(1^{F}_{J})

one has

p^{F}_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}

in
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}\rangle
;

(2^{F}_{J})

one has

\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}p^{F}_{K}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

Note that

(0^{F}_{J})
implies

p^{F}_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}G}=p^{G}_{J}

for
