.
Thus

(0^{F}_{J})
will yield

p^{F}_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{F}_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}G}

for all
B\in{\mathscr{A}}(F)
,
which means that

p^{F}_{J}
is fissile.
Thus
it will remain to put
p_{J}=p^{E}_{J}
.

Induction on
(F,J)\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(E)\times{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
.
Take a pair
(F,J)
.
We assume that

p^{G}_{K}
are defined
and
the conditions
(0^{G}_{K})
–
(2^{G}_{K})
are satisfied
for

(G,K)\in{\mathscr{P}}_{\times}(F)\times{\mathscr{P}}(J)\setminus\{(F,J)\}.

We
construct
p^{F}_{J}

and
check the conditions
(0^{F}_{J})
–
(2^{F}_{J})
.

For
B\in{\mathscr{A}}(F)
,
put

U(B)=\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

For
B,C\in{\mathscr{A}}(F)
,
B\geqslant C
,
we have,
by Lemma 17.1,
the restriction homomorphism

{?}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[C]}:U(B)\to U(C).

Thus we have a presheaf

U:{\mathscr{A}}(F)\to{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{Ab}}}.

By Lemma 17.1,
the canonical retractions

{\svb r}^{B}_{C}:{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]\to{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[C]

induce homomorphisms

\lambda^{C}_{B}=\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\svb r}^{B}_{C}}\rangle|_{U(C)\to U(B)}:U(C)\to U(B),

which form an extender for
U
,
as follows from Corollary 12.1.
For
B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)={\mathscr{A}}(F)\setminus\{\{F\}\}
,
introduce the ensemble
u_{B}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}\rangle
,

u_{B}=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}.

By Lemma 15.1,

u_{B}=\sum_{l\in{\mathscr{R}}(B,J)}\ \mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}\ \sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(l(G))}(-1)^{|l(G)|-|K|}p^{G}_{K}.

By
(2^{G}_{l(G)})
,
the inner sum belongs to

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}G}\rangle^{[|l(G)|]}
.
Using
Lemma 17.4
and
the inequality

\sum_{G\in B}|l(G)|\geqslant|J|,

we get that
the hash product
and
thus

u_{B}

belong to
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}\rangle^{[|J|]}
.
We have got

u_{B}\in U(B)
.
For

B,C\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)
,
B\geqslant C
,
and

K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)
,
we have

\bigl(\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}\bigr)\big|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[C]}=\hfill\text{(by naturality of $\mathbin{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sbighash$}}}$)}\hfill=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[C\wedge\{G\}]}=\qquad\\
\qquad\text{(by $(0^{G}_{K})$)}\hfill=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}\ \mathop{\sbighash}_{H\in C\wedge\{G\}}p^{H}_{K}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{H\in C}p^{H}_{K}.

It follows that
u_{B}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[C]}=u_{C}
,
that is,

(u_{B})_{B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)}\in\lim_{B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)}U(B).

By Lemma 9.2,
there exists an ensemble

u\in U(\{F\})=\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle^{[|J|]}

(18)

such that

u|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=u_{B},\qquad B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F).

Consider the ensembles
q,r\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle
,

q=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-1-|K|}p^{F}_{K},\qquad r=q+u.

For
B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)
,
we have

q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-1-|K|}p^{F}_{K}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\hfill\text{(by $(0^{F}_{K})$)}\qquad\\
=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-1-|K|}\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}

(19)

and

r|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}+u|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}+u_{B}=\hfill\text{(by \eqref{qB})}\qquad\\
=\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-1-|K|}\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}+\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{K}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{J}.

(20)

We have

r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}+\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{K}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=\hfill\text{(by $(1^{F}_{K})$)}\qquad\\
=r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}+\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}p^{F}_{K}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}=r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}-q|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}=\\
\qquad=u|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\in\hfill\text{(by Lemma\penalty\ 17.1)}\hfill\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

From
this
and
Lemma 19.2,

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

(21)

Choose
i\in I\setminus J
.
We have the filling homomorphism

\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\rangle\to\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle.

Put

p^{F}_{J}=r+\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}).

Check of
(0^{F}_{J})
.

For
B=\{F\}
,
the condition is satisfied trivially.
Take
B\in{\mathscr{A}}^{\times}(F)
.
We have

r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}}=\hfill\text{(by \eqref{rB} and naturality of $\mathbin{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sbighash$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sbighash$}}}$)}\hfill=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}G)_{+}}=\qquad\\
\qquad\text{(by $(1^{G}_{J})$)}\hfill=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{G}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}}.

(22)

By construction of
\chi^{J}_{i}
,
we have the commutative diagram

\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\chi^{J}_{i}}
\scriptstyle{{?}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{?}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\chi^{J}_{i}}
\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}.}

We get

p^{F}_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=r|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}+\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\\
\qquad\text{(by the diagram)}\hfill=r|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}+\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B])_{+}})=\qquad\\
\qquad\text{(by \eqref{rB-base})}\hfill=r|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[B]}=\hfill\text{(by \eqref{rB})}\hfill=\mathop{\sbighash}_{G\in B}p^{G}_{J}.

Check of
(1^{F}_{J})
.

We have

p^{F}_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}=\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}=\\
\qquad\text{(by \eqref{fill})}\hfill={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}.

Thus

p^{F}_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}
.

Check of
(2^{F}_{J})
.

It follows from (
21
)
by Lemma 19.1,
that

\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle^{[|J|]}.

(23)

We have

\sum_{K\in{\mathscr{P}}(J)}(-1)^{|J|-|K|}p^{F}_{K}=p^{F}_{J}-q=r+\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})-q=\\
=u+\langle\chi^{J}_{i}\rangle({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{F}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-r|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F)_{+}})\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{J}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle^{[|J|]},

where
\in
follows from (
18
) and (
23
).
∎

There exists an
(|I|-1)
-almost fissile ensemble

q\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle
such that

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{I}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-q|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}\rangle^{[|I|]}.

Lemma 19.3 gives fissile ensembles

p_{J}\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle

satisfying the conditions

(1)

and

(2)

thereof.
Put

q=\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}p_{J}.

Check that

q
is
(|I|-1)
-almost fissile.
Take
A\in{\mathscr{A}}(E)
.
We have

\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}-q|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}=\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}p_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}-\\
\qquad-\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}p_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}=\hfill\text{(since $p_{J}$ are fissile)}\qquad\\
=\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}p_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}-\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}\mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}p_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}=\\
\qquad\text{(by Lemma\penalty\ 15.2)}\hfill=\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{R}}^{\prime}(A,I)}\ \mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}\ \sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(k(F))}(-1)^{|k(F)|-|J|}p_{J}|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}=\qquad\\
=\sum_{k\in{\mathscr{R}}^{\prime}(A,I)}\ \mathop{\sbighash}_{F\in A}\bigl(\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(k(F))}(-1)^{|k(F)|-|J|}p_{J}\bigr)\big|_{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}.

By condition
(2)
,
the inner sum of the last expression belongs to

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle^{[|k(F)|]}
.
By Lemma 17.1,
its restriction to
{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F
belongs to

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}F}\rangle^{[|k(F)|]}
.
Using
Lemma 17.4
and
the inequality

\sum_{F\in A}|k(F)|\geqslant|I|,

we get that
the hash product
and
thus
the whole expression
belong to
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}[A]}\rangle^{[|I|]}
,
as required.

We have

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{I}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-q|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{I}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}p_{J}|_{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}=\\
\qquad\text{(by condition $(1)$)}\hfill={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{I}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}-\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-1-|J|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=\qquad\\
\qquad=\sum_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}(-1)^{|I|-|J|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}{\svb j}^{E}_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\in\hfill\text{(by Lemma\penalty\ 19.2)}\hfill\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{(\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E)_{+}}\rangle^{[|I|]}.

∎

§ 20. The filtration
\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle^{[s]}

We give a topological version of the definition of § 17.
Let

T

and

Z

be spaces.
Let a chained monoid
P
act on
Z

(preserving the basepoint).
The set
Z^{T}
carries the induced action of
P
.
Thus
the abelian group
\langle Z^{T}\rangle
becomes a module over
\langle P\rangle
.
We define a filtration
\langle Z^{T}\rangle^{[s]}
.
Let

T_{j}
,
j\in(n)
,
be spaces
and

f:T\to\bigvee_{j\in(n)}T_{j}

be a map.
Take ensembles
V_{j}\in\langle P\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle Z^{T_{j}}\rangle
,

j\in(n)
,
and
consider the ensemble
V\in\langle Z^{T}\rangle
,

V=\langle Z^{f}\rangle\bigl(\mathop{\sbighash}_{j\in(n)}V_{j}\bigr).

(24)

We call
V
a
block
of
rank

s_{1}+\dotso+s_{n}
.
We let
\langle Z^{T}\rangle^{[s]}\subseteq\langle Z^{T}\rangle
be the subgroup
generated by all blocks of rank at least
s
.
One easily sees that
it is a submodule.

Let

\widetilde{Z}
be a space
with an action of
P

and

h:Z\to\widetilde{Z}
be a
P
-equivariant map.
Then
the homomorphism

\langle h^{T}\rangle:\langle Z^{T}\rangle\to\langle{\widetilde{Z}}^{T}\rangle

takes
\langle Z^{T}\rangle^{[s]}
to
\langle{\widetilde{Z}}^{T}\rangle^{[s]}
.
∎

Let

\textstyle\sib T

and

\textstyle\sib Z

be pointed simplicial sets.
Let

P
act on

\textstyle\sib Z

and
thus
on
|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}|
.
Consider
the geometric realization function

\gamma:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|},\qquad{\sib v}\mapsto|{\sib v}|,

and
the homomorphism

\langle\gamma\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle.

Then

\langle\gamma\rangle
takes
\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}
to

\langle|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib Z$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib Z$}}}|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{[s]}
.
∎

The case
Z=Y^{X}
.

Let

I
be a finite set
and

Y
be a space
with an action of the chained monoid
P={\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
We suppose that
the action is
special
:

Y=\bigcup_{i\in I}\Fix\{i\}_{(Y)}.

Let
T
be a space.
The set
Y^{T}
carries the induced action of
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
Then,
in the
\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle
-module
\langle Y^{T}\rangle
,
the inclusion holds

\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[s]}\langle Y^{T}\rangle\subseteq\langle Y^{T}\rangle^{(s)}.

Take
a map
u\in Y^{T}

and
a subset
J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
|J|\geqslant s
.
The ensembles of the form
\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}
generate the subgroup

\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[s]}\langle Y^{T}\rangle
.
Thus
we should show that

\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\in\langle Y^{T}\rangle^{(s)}
.
Take a subspace
R\in{\mathscr{F}}_{s-1}(T)
.
We should check that

\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}|_{R}=0

in
\langle Y^{R}\rangle
.
Since the action is special,
for each
t\in T
,
there is
i_{t}\in I
such that

u(t)\in\Fix\{i_{t}\}_{(Y)}
.
Consider the subset

K=\{\,i_{t}\mid t\in R\setminus\{{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}\}\}\in{\mathscr{P}}(I).

Clearly,

|K|<s
.
For
t\in R\setminus\{{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}\}
,
we have

K_{(Y)}(u(t))=K_{(Y)}(\{i_{t}\}_{(Y)}(u(t)))=\\
=(K\cap\{i_{t}\})_{(Y)}(u(t))=\{i_{t}\}_{(Y)}(u(t))=u(t).

Thus

K_{(Y)}\circ u\mathrel{{=}|_{R}}u
.
Thus

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}K{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\mathrel{{=}|_{R}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}

in
\langle Y^{R}\rangle
.
Since
|K|<s
,
we have
K\nsupseteq J
.
It follows that

\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}K{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=0

in
\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle
.
We get

\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\mathrel{{=}|_{R}}\omega_{J}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}K{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}u{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{0.21529pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}=0.\displaymath@qed

Let
X
be a compact Hausdorff space.
Consider the space
Z=Y^{X}
.
It carries the induced action of
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.

Let
T
be a space.
Then

\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle^{[s]}\subseteq\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle^{(s)}_{X}.

Take a block
V\in\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle
of rank at least
s
.
We should show that

V\in\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle^{(s)}_{X}
.
Consider the homomorphism

\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle\xrightarrow{\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle}\langle Y^{T\wedge X}\rangle.

By Lemma 3.3,
we should show that

\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V)\in\langle Y^{T\wedge X}\rangle^{(s)}
.
We have the equality (
24
)
for some
spaces
T_{j}
,
map
f

and
ensembles
V_{j}\in\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle(Y^{X})^{T_{j}}\rangle
,
where
s_{1}+\dots s_{n}\geqslant s
.
Since the function

{\widehat{\square}}^{X}:(Y^{X})^{T_{j}}\to Y^{T_{j}\wedge X}

is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant,

\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V_{j})\in\langle{\mathscr{P}}(I)\rangle^{[s_{j}]}\langle Y^{T_{j}\wedge X}\rangle
.
By Lemma 20.3,

\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V_{j})\in\langle Y^{T_{j}\wedge X}\rangle^{(s_{j})}.

Consider the commutative diagram

\textstyle{{\scriptstyle(V_{j})_{j\in(n)}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle(\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V_{j}))_{j\in(n)}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\prod\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle(Y^{X})^{T_{j}}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\prod\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}}
\textstyle{\prod\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle Y^{T_{j}\wedge X}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}V_{j}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\langle(Y^{X})^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}T_{j}}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\langle(Y^{X})^{f}\rangle}
\textstyle{\langle Y^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}T_{j}\wedge X}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\langle Y^{f\wedge{\mathrm{id}}_{X}}\rangle}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\mathop{\sbighash}\limits_{j\in(n)}\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V_{j})}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{(*)}
\textstyle{\langle(Y^{X})^{T}\rangle\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle}
\textstyle{\langle Y^{T\wedge X}\rangle.}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle V}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V)}}

(We used distributivity of the smash product over the wedge.)
All the sendings are obvious
except
(*)
,
which follows by commutativity of the diagram.
By
[
3
, Lemma 3.1]
,

\mathop{\sbighash}_{j\in(n)}\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V_{j})\in\langle Y^{\bigvee_{j\in(n)}T_{j}\wedge X}\rangle^{(s)}.

By
[
3
, Lemma 2.1]
,

\langle{\widehat{\square}}^{X}\rangle(V)\in\langle Y^{T\wedge X}\rangle^{(s)}
,
as was to be shown.
∎

§ 21. The wedge
V(I)
and a
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant map

h:V(I)\to Z

Let
I
be a finite set.
We give a topological version of
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)
.
Consider the space

V(I)=\bigvee_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J).

Let

{\mathrm{in}}_{J}:{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\to V(I)

be the canonical insertions.

V(I)
consists of
the isolated
lead
point

\top_{V(I)}={\mathrm{in}}_{I}(1_{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta\varnothing})

and
the subspace

V^{\times}(I)=\bigvee_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)}{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J),

which is
contractible.

For
J,K\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
J\supseteq K
,
let

\tau^{J}_{K}:{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\to{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K)

be the map induced by the inclusion

I\setminus J\to I\setminus K
.

Let the monoid
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
act on
V(I)
by the rule

\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{J}_{K\cap J}}
\scriptstyle{{\mathrm{in}}_{J}}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus(K\cap J))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{{\mathrm{in}}_{K\cap J}}
\textstyle{V(I)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{K_{(V(I))}}
\textstyle{V(I),}

K\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
The subspace
V^{\times}(I)
is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-invariant.

For
J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
we have the map

e_{J}:|{\boldsymbol{\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr\Sigma$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)|={\overline{\Sigma}}|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr E$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr E$}}}(I\setminus J)|\xrightarrow{{\overline{\Sigma}}\xi_{I\setminus J}}{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)

(see (
8
) for
\xi_{I\setminus J}
).
These
e_{J}
form the map

e=\bigvee_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}e_{J}:|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|\to V(I).

(25)

It
is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant,
sends the point
|\top_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)}|
to
\top_{V(I)}
,
and
takes the subspace
|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)|
to
V^{\times}(I)
.

Let
Z
be a space
with an action of
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
Suppose that
the basepoint path component
Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}\subseteq Z
is weakly
contractible.
Let
\top_{Z}\in Z
be a point such that

K_{(Z)}(\top_{Z})\in Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}

for all
K\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
.
Then
there exists a
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant map
h:V(I)\to Z
such
that

h(\top_{V(I)})=\top_{Z}
.

We
crop
Z

and
assume that

Z=Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}\cup\{\top_{Z}\}
.
We will construct maps

h^{J}:{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\to Z,\qquad J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I),

satisfying the following conditions

(1)

and

(2^{K}_{J})

for
J,K\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
J\subseteq K
:

(1)

one has
h^{I}(1_{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta\varnothing})=\top_{Z}
;

(2^{K}_{J})

the diagram

\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{h^{K}}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{K}_{J}}
\textstyle{Z\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{J_{(Z)}}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{h^{J}}
\textstyle{Z}

is commutative.

Note that
the condition
(2^{J}_{J})

is the equality
J_{(Z)}\circ h^{J}=h^{J}
.

Induction on
J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
We define the map
h^{I}
by the condition
(1)
.
The condition
(2^{I}_{I})
is satisfied trivially.
Take
J\in{\mathscr{P}}^{\times}(I)
.
We assume that
the maps
h^{K}
are defined
for
K\supsetneq J

and
the conditions
(2^{L}_{K})
are satisfied
for
L\supseteq K\supsetneq J
.
We
construct
h^{J}

and check
(2^{K}_{J})

for
K\supseteq J
.

For
K\supsetneq J
,
put

B_{K}=\Im({\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K)\xrightarrow{\tau^{K}_{J}}{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)).

Since
\tau^{K}_{J}
is an embedding,
there is a map
f^{K}:B_{K}\to Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}
such that

f^{K}(\tau^{K}_{J}(t))=J_{(Z)}(h^{K}(t)),\qquad t\in{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K),

(we use here that

\Im J_{(Z)}\subseteq Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}
).
We show that

f^{K}\mathrel{{=}|_{B_{K}\cap B_{L}}}f^{L}

for
K,L\supsetneq J
.
Take
s\in B_{K}\cap B_{L}
.
Since
B_{K}\cap B_{L}=B_{K\cup L}
,
we have
s=\tau^{K\cup L}_{J}(t)

for some
t\in{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus(K\cup L))
.
We have the commutative diagram

\textstyle{{\scriptstyle t}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus(K\cup L))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{h^{K\cup L}}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{K\cup L}_{J}}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{{K\cup L}}_{K}}
\textstyle{Z\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{K_{(Z)}}
\textstyle{{\scriptstyle s}}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{K}_{J}}
\scriptstyle{h^{K}}
\textstyle{Z}

(the square is commutative by
(2^{K\cup L}_{K})
).
Using the diagram,
we get

f^{K}(s)=f^{K}(\tau^{K\cup L}_{J}(t))=f^{K}(\tau^{K}_{J}(\tau^{K\cup L}_{K}(t)))=J_{(Z)}(h^{K}(\tau^{K\cup L}_{K}(t)))=\\
=J_{(Z)}(K_{(Z)}(h^{K\cup L}(t)))=(J\cap K)_{(Z)}(h^{K\cup L}(t))=J_{(Z)}(h^{K\cup L}(t)).

Similarly,

f^{L}(s)=J_{(Z)}(h^{K\cup L}(t))
.
Thus

f^{K}(s)=f^{L}(s)
,
as promised.

We have

\bigcup_{K\supsetneq J}B_{K}={\overline{\Sigma}}\partial\Delta(I\setminus J)\subseteq{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J),

where
\partial\Delta(I\setminus J)
denotes
the boundary of the simplex
\Delta(I\setminus J)
.
Since
B_{K}
are closed,
there is a map

f:{\overline{\Sigma}}\partial\Delta(I\setminus J)\to Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}

such that

f|_{B_{K}}=f^{K}

for all
K\supsetneq J
.
Since

{\overline{\Sigma}}\partial\Delta(I\setminus J)
is the boundary
of the ball
{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)

and

Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}
is weakly contractible,

f
extends to a map

g:{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\to Z_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}.

We put

h^{J}(s)=J_{(Z)}(g(s)),\qquad s\in{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J).

Clearly,

J_{(Z)}\circ h^{J}=h^{J}
,
which is the condition
(2^{J}_{J})
.
We check the condition
(2^{K}_{J})

for
K\supsetneq J
.
For
t\in{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus K)
,
we have

h^{J}(\tau^{K}_{J}(t))=J_{(Z)}(g(\tau^{K}_{J}(t)))=J_{(Z)}(f(\tau^{K}_{J}(t)))=J_{(Z)}(f^{K}(\tau^{K}_{J}(t)))=\\
=J_{(Z)}(J_{(Z)}(h^{K}(t)))=J_{(Z)}(h^{K}(t)),

as required.

We join all the
h^{J}
into the desired

:

h=\mathop{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hrule height=0.86108pt\vskip 0.99025pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.99025pt\hrule height=0.86108pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.6458pt\vskip 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 1.07639pt\hrule height=0.6458pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.55974pt\vskip 0.90419pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.90419pt\hrule height=0.55974pt}}{\vbox{\hrule height=0.38748pt\vskip 0.6458pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bigvee$}\vskip 0.6458pt\hrule height=0.38748pt}}}}_{J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)}h^{J}.

Since
\top_{V(I)}={\mathrm{in}}_{I}(1_{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta\varnothing})
,
we have

h(\top_{V(I)})=h^{I}(1_{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta\varnothing})=\hfill\text{(by $(1)$)}\hfill=\top_{Z}.

To show that

h
is
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant,
we should check that,
for
K,J\in{\mathscr{P}}(I)
,
the diagram

\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus J)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{h^{J}}
\scriptstyle{\tau^{J}_{K\cap J}}
\textstyle{Z\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{K_{(Z)}}
\textstyle{{\overline{\Sigma}}\Delta(I\setminus(K\cap J))\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{h^{K\cap J}}
\textstyle{Z}

is commutative.
Indeed,

K_{(Z)}\circ h^{J}=\hfill\text{(by $(2^{J}_{J})$)}\hfill=K_{(Z)}\circ J_{(Z)}\circ h^{J}=\qquad\\
\qquad=(K\cap J)_{(Z)}\circ h^{J}=\hfill\text{(by $(2^{J}_{K\cap J})$)}\hfill=h^{K\cap J}\circ\tau^{J}_{K\cap J}.\displaymath@qed

§ 22. The realization

\Upsilon_{h}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}

Let

X

and

Y

be spaces,

X
compact Hausdorff.
Let

I
be a finite set
and

Y
carry a special action of the monoid
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
.
Let
h:V(I)\to Y^{X}
be a
{\mathscr{P}}(I)
-equivariant map.
Let

\textstyle\sib T

be a pointed simplicial set.
Introduce the function

\Upsilon_{h}=\Upsilon^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}_{h}:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|},\qquad{\sib v}\mapsto(|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|\xrightarrow{|v|}|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|\xrightarrow{e}V(I)\xrightarrow{h}Y^{X}),

(see (
25
) for
e
),
the
realization
.

The function
\Upsilon_{h}
takes

{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}

to

(Y^{X}_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}
.

The map
e
takes
|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}^{\times}(I)|
to
V^{\times}(I)
.
Since
V^{\times}(I)
is path connected,

h
takes it to
Y^{X}_{\mathchoice{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\displaystyle\raisebox{4.2625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\boldsymbol{<}$}\mkern-4.1mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}{\scriptstyle\raisebox{1.53706pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}\mkern-4.4mu{|}}}
.
∎

Consider the homomorphism

\langle\Upsilon_{h}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\to\langle(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle.

The homomorphism
\langle\Upsilon_{h}\rangle
takes

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{(s)}_{X}
.

We have the decomposition

\langle\Upsilon_{h}\rangle:\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\langle\gamma\rangle}\langle|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle\xrightarrow{\langle(h\circ e)^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle}\langle(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle,

where
\gamma:{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\to|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}
is the geometric
realization function.
By Lemma 20.2,

\langle\gamma\rangle
takes

\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{[s]}
.
By Lemma 20.1,

\langle(h\circ e)^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle
takes

\langle|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)|^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{[s]}

to

\langle(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{[s]}
,
which is contained in

\langle(Y^{X})^{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib T$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib T$}}}|}\rangle^{(s)}_{X}

by Lemma 20.4.
∎

Let

E
be a nonempty finite set
and

q\in\langle{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sib W$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sib W$}}}(I)^{{\boldsymbol{\check{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sibr C$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sibr C$}}}}}\b{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.21}{$\displaystyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\textstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}{\scalebox{1.21}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sir\Delta$}}}E}\rangle
be an
r
-almost fissile ensemble.
Then
the ensemble

\langle\Upsilon_{h}\rangle(q)\in\langle(Y^{X})^{{\check{\mathrm{C}}}\Delta E}\rangle
is

(X,r)
-almost fissile.

.

Since
